return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during  if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Archive for JADE-style mutation\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n\n        # JADE-style adaptive state\n        archive = []\n        max_archive_size = max(pop_size, 100)\n        p_best_frac = 0.2\n        c_update = 0.1\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # JADE-style mutation with archive and optional pbest selection\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            # Sort indices by fitness (smaller is better)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            # Use top pbest_rate% as pbest pool, minimum 2\n            pbest_rate = float(params.get('pbest_rate', 0.2))\n            pbest_n = max(2, int(pbest_rate * pop_size))\n            archive_prob = float(params.get('archive_prob', 0.5))\n            \n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual dithering of F and CR for better exploration\n                F_i = float(np.clip(F + 0.1 * (rng.random() - 0.5), 0.1, 1.0))\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR + 0.1 * (rng.random() - 0.5), 0.0, 1.0))\n                \n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_n\n                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\n                if pbest_idx == i and pbest_n > 1:\n                    # Pick another pbest index to avoid using current as pbest\n                    pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                \n                # Choose two distinct indices != i from population\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) < 2:\n                    candidates = list(range(pop_size))\n                a_idx, b_idx = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\n                a = pop[a_idx]\n                b = pop[b_idx]\n                \n                # With probability archive_prob, use archive vector as b for extra diversity\n                if archive.shape[0] >= 1 and rng.random() < archive_prob:\n                    b = archive[rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0])]\n                \n                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (a - b)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = np.copy(pop[i])\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n                \n                # Boundary handling: clip and optionally                  done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with JADE archive update\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive the replaced (old) solution for diversity\n                    if archive.shape[0] >= pop_size:\n                        archive[rng.randint(0, pop_size)] = pop[i].copy()\n                    else:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].copy()])\n in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n        self.total_reward += reward\n        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n        \n        if not self.training:\"\n    }\n    </json>",

\u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n\n        def forward(self, x):\n            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n            return self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            self.load(model_path)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n        \n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n        else:\n            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n\n        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n        else:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n\n        if self.training:\n            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n            self._last_obs = observation\n            self._last_action_idx = idx\n            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n\n        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n\n    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n        self.total_reward += reward\n        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n        \n        if not self.training:\"\n    }\n    </json>"

return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if  if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, JADE-style mutation with archive and optional pbest selection\n   142\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   143\t            # Sort indices by fitness (smaller is better)\n   144\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   145\t            # Choose two distinct indices != i from population\n   163\t                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   164\t                if len(candidates) < 2:\n   165\t                    candidates = list(range(pop_size))\n   166\t                a_idx, b_idx = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\n   167\t                a = pop[a_idx]\n   168\t                b = pop[b_idx]\n   169\t                \n   170\t                # Boundary handling: clip and optionally reflect\n   185\t                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n   186\t            \n   187\t            # Evaluate trial vectors\n   188\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   189\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   190\t                    done = True\n   191\t                    break\n   192\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   193\t                evals += 1\n   194\t                \n   195\t                # Greedy selection with JADE archive update\n   196\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   197\t                    # Archive the replaced (old) solution for diversity\n   198\t                    if archive.shape[0] >= pop_size:\n   199\t                        archive[rng.randint(0, pop_size)] = pop[i].copy()\n   200\t                    else:\n   201\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].copy()])\n   202\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   203\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   204\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   205\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   206\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   207\t            \n   208\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   209\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   210\t            \n   211\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   212\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   213\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   214\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   215\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   216\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   217\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   218\t            \n   219\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   220\t\n   221\t        self.stats = {\n   222\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   223\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   224\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   225\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   226\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   227\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   228\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   229\t        }\n   230\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

Use top pbest_rate% as pbest pool, minimum 2\\n            pbest_rate = float(params.get('pbest_rate', 0.2))\\n            pbest_n = max(2, int(pbest_rate * pop_size))\\n            archive_prob = float(params.get('archive_prob', 0.5))\\n            \\n            used_F = np.empty(pop_size)\\n            used_CR = np.empty(pop_size)\\n            improved_mask = np.zeros(pop_size, dtype=bool)\\n            \\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Sample SHADE-adapted F and CR from memory, with fallback to controller values\\n                mi = int(rng.randint(0, memory_size))\\n                if memory_F[mi] > 0:\\n                    # Cauchy distribution centered at memory_F[mi], clipped to [0,1]\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + memory_F[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\\n                else:\\n                    F_i = float(F_base)\\n                \\n                if memory_CR[mi] > 0:\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.normal(memory_CR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                else:\\n                    CR_i = float(CR_base)\\n                \\n                used_F[i] = F_i\\n                used_CR[i] = CR_i\\n                \\n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_n\\n                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\\n                if pbest_idx == i and pbest_n > 1:\\n                    pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n                \\n                # Choose two distinct indices != i from population\\n                candidates = np.array([idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i])\\n                if candidates.size < 2:\\n                    candidates = np.arange(pop_size)\\n                a_idx, b_idx = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\\n                a = pop[a_idx]\\n                b = pop[b_idx]\\n                \\n                # Optionally use archive vector as b for extra diversity\\n                if archive.shape[0] >= 1 and rng.random() < archive_prob:\\n                    b = archive[rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0])]\\n                \\n                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (a - b)\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                trial = np.copy(pop[i])\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: reflect then clip (keeps diversity at bounds)\\n                reflected = np.where((trial < lb) | (trial > ub),\\n                                     2.0 * np.where(trial < lb, lb, ub) - trial,\\n                                     trial)\\n                trial = np.clip(reflected, lb, ub)\\n                new_pop[i] = trial\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection with JADE archive update\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    improved_mask[i] = True\\n                    # Archive the replaced old solution for diversity\\n                    if archive.shape[0] >= max_archive_size:\\n                        archive[rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0])] = pop[i].copy()\\n                    else:\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].copy()])\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Record successful F/CR values for SHADE memory update\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if improved_mask[i]:\\n                    success_F.append(used_F[i])\\n                    success_CR.append(used_CR[i])\\n            \\n            # SHADE-style memory update using Lehmer mean for F and arithmetic mean for CR\\n            if len(success_F) > 0:\\n                sF = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\\n                sCR = np.asarray(success_CR, dtype=float)\\n                w = np.abs(sF) / max(np.sum(np.abs(sF)), 1e-12)\\n                lehmer_F = float(np.sum(w * sF * sF) / max(np.sum(w * sF), 1e-12))\\n                mean_CR = float(np.mean(sCR))\\n                memory_F[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(lehmer_F, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                memory_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_size\\n                success_F = []\\n                success_CR = []\\n            \\n            # Track stagnation for future diversity reinitialization\\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f:\\n                stagnation_counter = 0\\n            else:\\n                stagnation_counter += 1\"\n    }\n}\n</json>"

SHADE/JADE-style mutation with archive and adaptive F/CR memory\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            # Sort indices by fitness (smaller is better)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            # Use top pbest_rate% as pbest pool, minimum 2\n            pbest_rate = float(params.get('pbest_rate', 0.2))\n            pbest_n = max(2, int(pbest_rate * pop_size))\n            archive_prob = float(params.get('archive_prob', 0.5))\n            \n            used_F = np.empty(pop_size)\n            used_CR = np.empty(pop_size)\n            improved_mask = np.zeros(pop_size, dtype=bool)\n            \n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_n\n                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\n                if pbest_idx == i and pbest_n > 1:\n                    pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                \n                # Choose two distinct indices != i from population\n                candidates = np.array([idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i])\n                if candidates.size < 2:\n                    candidates = np.arange(pop_size)\n                a_idx, b_idx = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\n                a = pop[a_idx]\n                b = pop[b_idx]\n                \n                # Optionally use archive vector as b for extra diversity\n                if archive.shape[0] >= 1 and rng.random() < archive_prob:\n                    b = archive[rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0])]\n                \n                # Mutation: current-to-pbest/1\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (a - b)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = np.copy(pop[i])\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n                \n                # Boundary handling: reflect then clip (keeps diversity at bounds)\n                reflected = np.where((trial < lb) | (trial > ub),\n                                     2.0 * np.where(trial < lb, lb, ub) - trial,\n                                     trial)\n                trial = np.clip(reflected, lb, ub)\n                new_pop[i] = trial\n            \n            # Evaluate trial vectors\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with JADE archive update\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    improved_mask[i] = True\n                    # Archive the replaced old solution for diversity\n                    if archive.shape[0] >= max_archive_size:\n                        archive[rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0])] = pop[i].copy()\n                    else:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].copy()])\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n            \n            # Record successful F/CR values for SHADE memory update\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if improved_mask[i]:\n                    success_F.append(used_F[i])\n                    success_CR.append(used_CR[i])\n            \n            # SHADE-style memory update using Lehmer mean for F and arithmetic mean for CR\n            if len(success_F) > 0:\n                sF = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\n                sCR = np.asarray(success_CR, dtype=float)\n                w = np.abs(sF) / max(np.sum(np.abs(sF)), 1e-12)\n                lehmer_F = float(np.sum(w * sF * sF) / max(np.sum(w * sF), 1e-12))\n                mean_CR = float(np.mean(sCR))\n                memory_F[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(lehmer_F, 0.0, 1.0))\n                memory_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n                memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_size\n                success_F = []\n                success_CR = []\n            \n            # Track stagnation for adaptive population reduction / restart\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f:\n                stagnation_counter = 0\n            else:\n                stagnation_counter += 1\n\n            # L-SHADE style population size reduction on prolonged stagnation\n            if stagnation_counter >= 20 and pop_size > 8:\n                fresh_sorted = np.argsort(fitness)\n                keep_n = max(4, int(pop_size // 2))\n                keep_idx = fresh_sorted[:keep_n]\n                pop = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n                fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n                pop_size = keep_n\n                # Keep the archive intact for diversity; it may already be large enough.\n                stagnation_counter = 0\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\"\n    }\n    </json>",

'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Archive for JADE/SHADE-style mutation\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n        max_archive_size = max(2 * pop_size, 100)\n\n        # SHADE-style memory for F and CR adaptation\n        memory_size = 5\n        memory_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\n        memory_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\n        memory_pos = 0\n\n        # Successful parameter records for memory update\n        success_F = []\n        success_CR = []\n\n        # Stagnation tracking\n        stagnation_counter = 0\n        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F_base = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR_base = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # SHADE/JADE-style mutation with archive and adaptive F/CR memory\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            # Sort indices by fitness (smaller is better)\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            # Use top pbest_rate% as pbest pool, minimum 2\n            pbest_rate = float(params.get('pbest_rate', 0.2))\n            pbest_n = max(2, int(pbest_rate * pop_size))\n            archive_prob = float(params.get('archive_prob', 0.5))\n            \n            used_F = np.empty(pop_size)\n            used_CR = np.empty(pop_size)\n            improved_mask = np.zeros(pop_size, dtype=bool)\n            \n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample SHADE-adapted F and CR from memory, with fallback to controller values\n                mi = int(rng.randint(0, memory_size))\n                if memory_F[mi] > 0:\n                    # Cauchy distribution centered at memory_F[mi], clipped to [0,1]\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + memory_F[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n                else:\n                    F_i = float(F_base)\n                \n                if memory_CR[mi] > 0:\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.normal(memory_CR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\n                else:\n                    CR_i = float(CR_base)\n                \n                used_F[i] = F_i\n                used_CR[i] = CR_i\n                \n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_n\n                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\n                if pbest_idx == i and pbest_n > 1:\n                    pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_n)]\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                \n                # Choose candidate index pool (exclude current index i)\n                candidates = np.array([idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i])\n                if candidates.size < 3:\n                    candidates = np.arange(pop_size)\n                \n                strategy = str(params.get('strategy', 'jade'))\n                if strategy == 'rand/1':\n                    # Classic rand/1 mutation: v = x_r1 + F*(x_r2 - x_r3)\n                    r1, r2, r3 = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n                    mutant = pop[r1] + F_i * (pop[r2] - pop[r3])\n                else:\n                    # Choose two distinct indices != i from population\n                    if candidates.size < 2:\n                        candidates = np.arange(pop_size)\n                    a_idx, b_idx = rng.choice(candidates, size=2, replace=False)\n                    a = pop[a_idx]\n                    b = pop[b_idx]\n                    \n                    # Optionally use archive vector as b for extra diversity\n current-to-pbest/1\n                    mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (a - b)\n                \n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                trial = np.copy(pop[i])\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n                \n                # Boundary handling: reflect then clip (keeps diversity at bounds)\n                reflected = np.where((trial < lb) | (trial > ub),\n     trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with JADE archive update\n                if trial_f < success_F.append(used_F[i])\n                    success_CR.append(used_CR[i])\n            \n            # SHADE-style memory update using Lehmer mean for F and arithmetic mean for CR\n            if len(success_F) > 0:\n                sF = np.asarray(success_F, dtype=float)\n                sCR = np.asarray(success_CR, dtype=float)\n                w = np.abs(sF) / max(np.sum(np.abs(sF)), 1e-12)\n                lehmer_F = float(np.sum(w * sF * sF) / max(np.sum(w * sF), 1e-12))\n                mean_CR = float(np.mean(sCR))\n                memory_F[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(lehmer_F, 0.0, 1.0))\n                memory_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n                memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_size\n                success_F = []\n                success_CR = []\n            \n            # Track stagnation for adaptive population reduction / restart\n            if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f:\n                stagnation_counter = 0\n            else:\n                stagnation_counter += 1\n\n            # L-SHADE style population size reduction on prolonged stagnation\n            if stagnation_counter = pop[keep_idx].copy()\n                fitness = fitness[keep_idx].copy()\n                pop_size = keep_n\n                # Keep the archive intact for diversity; it may already be large enough.\n                stagnation_counter = 0\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            #
